## Applicable design — C-88 verbatim quotations

`$HOME/Workspace/dotfiles/.orchestration/plans/024-design-quality.md`, section 5.14, is uncommitted design input. The relied-on sentences of items 1, 2 and 9 are quoted verbatim below; the specific PermissionRequest uv contract in this task is the requested binding design.

> The protected-path inventory a seat's Edit denies cover is enumerated from the inputs of every trusted executable, not only the executables: it includes `~/.agents/permgate-policy.yaml` (permgate rereads it for every decision), `~/.local/lib/python*/site-packages` and every other user-site or startup-hook location of the interpreters in use (`.pth`, `usercustomize.py`, `sitecustomize.py`), `~/.claude-worker`'s hook and settings files, and the rendered guard; a text that binds an executable names its inputs in the same inventory (P1-15 amendment 5 C-80, 2026-10-19).
>
> A shell script that runs under a PR-controlled or worker-controlled environment (verdict.sh, the chain glue, CI steps) is executed as a program with `#!/bin/bash -p`, never as `bash <file>`, and only by a trusted parent that supplies the clean environment before the exec: `/usr/bin/env -i PATH=/usr/bin:/bin HOME=<passwd home> LANG=C.UTF-8 /bin/bash --noprofile --norc -p <absolute script path> …` (long options before `-p`). Privileged mode discards inherited functions, `BASH_ENV`/`ENV`, `SHELLOPTS`, `BASHOPTS`, `CDPATH` and `GLOBIGNORE`, but on Linux an inherited `LD_PRELOAD`/`LD_AUDIT` already executes while the loader maps `/bin/bash`, before `-p` or any statement runs, so a self re-exec inside the script is not a boundary and re-executing `"$0"` would reopen the caller's pathname (a worker-owned symlink can be swapped between the two executions); the program therefore verifies and refuses instead of repairing: its environment must consist of exactly the allowed names (checked through absolute paths `/usr/bin/env`, `/usr/bin/grep`), `$0` resolved with `realpath` must be a regular file under the repository root, and HOME must equal the passwd home of the real uid (`/usr/bin/dscl . -read /Users/<user> NFSHomeDirectory` on macOS, `/usr/bin/getent passwd <uid>` on Linux), otherwise exit 2 with a named message. A caller-set sentinel variable is not a sanitization proof, because the caller controls it (ci-efficiency a04 and verdict a04 C-80 reviews, 2026-10-19). No script accepts a caller-supplied executable directory; interpreters are the fixed absolute paths item 9 names.
>
> One trusted interpreter resolver (`scripts/orchestrator/trusted-interpreter.sh`, P1-3 lineage) resolves `<abs>` from the repository's pinned declaration to a regular file under the tool manager's install root (the install root is anchored to the passwd home of the real uid, never to the HOME variable, which a caller can forge), refuses anything else, and every orchestrator-side script consumes it; no script keeps its own `command -v`, `mise which` or PATH fallback (ci-rerun.sh security review 44cb835, 2026-10-19). Shell code of other projects (the agmsg storage layer and its `drivers.partition` sourcing) is never sourced by an orchestrator-side mechanism; counters are read from the agmsg SQLite store read-only with a fixed query or from the pasted record. Every Python child of a trusted entrypoint runs with `-I` (isolated: no user site, no `PYTHON*` environment, no script directory on `sys.path`), so `uv run … python -I <script>` rather than `--script` for a script without declared dependencies; a test plants a `.pth` and a `usercustomize.py` in a fake user site and asserts they never execute (P1-15 amendment 5 C-80, 2026-10-19). A program takes `UV_CACHE_DIR` from the environment the trusted parent supplies and refuses when it is absent or not an absolute path; it never computes a default from its own root. The orchestrator's parent prefix sets the main checkout's `.agents/worklog/uv-cache`; a worker's launcher sets `<worktree>/.uv-cache` (the P1-15 worker launch value: `.agents/` is read-only in the Codex permission profile, so a root-derived `.agents/worklog/uv-cache` cannot be created by a worker; P1-12 a03, 2026-10-19T11:28Z); tests set a temporary directory.

## Implementation contract

Declare uv 0.13.1 at .local/share/mise/installs/uv/0.13.1/uv-aarch64-apple-darwin/uv, Python 3.15.0 at .local/share/mise/installs/python/3.15.0/bin/python3.15, and node 26.12.0 at .local/share/mise/installs/node/26.12.0/bin/node, all relative to home. A test compares all three exact versions to home/dot_mise/config.toml. No wildcard, version alias, latest, shim, traversal or missing declaration is accepted. If the accepted pins differ from these probes, stop for a re-probed amendment rather than substituting an installed version.

Rendering composes {{ .chezmoi.homeDir }} with those declarations, validates shape only and is deterministic on a host with no installs. Use the same native expansion in both modify-script consumers and the validator renderer, with correct JSON and shell quoting, including homes with spaces. Do not add filesystem discovery or deployment requirements to ordinary rendering, statusline parsing or CI.

The managed PermissionRequest command is exactly this canonical raw form after rendering the host bindings, with shell-quoted absolute operands, literal double quotes on the two Claude variable expansions and the parent-selected private cache:

`/usr/bin/env -i PATH=/usr/bin:/bin HOME=<rendered passwd-home> LANG=C.UTF-8 TERM=dumb CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR="${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR}" CLAUDE_ENV_FILE="${CLAUDE_ENV_FILE}" UV_CACHE_DIR=<private cache> /bin/bash --noprofile --norc -p <abs trusted launch> <abs uv> run --no-config --no-project --isolated --offline --python <abs python> python -I <abs permgate> claude`

Replace the validator's literal permgate-claude substring rule with exact string equality between the raw command and that complete canonical template after rendering host bindings; both modify scripts use the same quote-preserving comparison for bound-form classification. Do not compare shlex tokens or normalize quoting, whitespace or variable expansions. In particular, unquoted CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR=${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR} is refused even though shlex produces the same tokens as the quoted form. Refuse missing, reordered or extra tokens, --script, nonisolated Python, shim paths and shell suffixes. Retain ccgate refusal, hook order, duplicate detection, synchronous timeout budgets and onFailure block.

Both modify scripts classify the legacy managed form separately and this exact raw bound form with its quotes preserved, replace stale managed hooks once, preserve custom hooks and converge idempotently. Keep each surface's P1-15 event inventory; do not import live orchestrator settings or add an event to a surface that lacks it. Run the existing ClaudeSettingsMergeTest legacy/custom fixtures unchanged.

Every scoped trusted entrypoint and test subject is in this single inventory; the render regression resolves every executable and interpreter in each actual rendered invocation under PATH=/usr/bin:/bin:

- PermissionRequest: /usr/bin/env, /bin/bash --noprofile --norc -p and the canonical trusted-launch.sh parent, declared real uv validated by that parent before exec, declared Python with -I, and the unchanged installed permgate script with the claude argument.
- Deployment: the same trusted-launch.sh parent and isolated/offline uv/Python, with cached --with pyyaml, running validate-agent-assets.py --check-effective-settings; its generated-config check child re-enters that parent and runs <abs uv> run --no-config --no-project --isolated --offline --with pyyaml --python <abs python> python -I scripts/generate-agent-configs.py --check, with the repository cwd fixed and the same private UV_CACHE_DIR. Never substitute the resolver's base Python directly for this yaml-importing child.
- Fixture subjects: the same trusted-launch.sh parent, those real hook binaries and permgate bytes, generate-agent-configs.py, validate-agent-assets.py and both modify scripts; Python subprocesses use the explicitly bound interpreter with -I, and every bound child importing yaml uses the same offline --with pyyaml uv pattern and private cache.
- Formatter interpreter check: the same trusted-launch.sh parent starts declared real node executing the probed installed Prettier script; Makefile format PATH includes node's declared bin directory so its env-node shebang resolves.

Add the opt-in --check-effective-settings mode, a concrete trusted launch in Makefile and the new executable scripts/orchestrator/trusted-launch.sh with English shdoc comments and #!/bin/bash -p. This is the named parent for every scoped bound command above, including the hook, checker, generator child, fixture subjects and formatter probe. Its trusted caller supplies the clean environment before exec through /bin/bash --noprofile --norc -p <abs trusted launch>; the parent verifies the exact accepted environment, canonical non-symlinked script pathname under the protected repository root and real-uid passwd HOME, and refuses rather than self-re-executing. Consume Python from the existing resolver under its restricted clean prefix before starting uv. Before any uv execution, including --version, the parent resolves the declared uv path and requires an executable regular file under <passwd home>/.local/share/mise/installs/uv/<exact pinned version>/, with no symlink at the declared path or redirected installation component; reject worktree, manifest worker roots, worker-writable caches and $TMPDIR containment after resolution. Run only the verified realpath and canonical bound argv, without PATH discovery, caller-selected executable directories or arbitrary command forwarding. On refusal emit exactly one stderr line trusted-launch: refused: <code>, no child output and exit 2: dirty-environment, script-path, home-mismatch, cache-path or uv-path for the corresponding failed entry, cache or uv identity/containment check; propagate resolver refusals without fallback. The checker must not bootstrap through bare uv, an inherited venv, PATH discovery or its own untrusted child. Ordinary validate-agent-assets and render-check stay pure. The opt-in mode reads effective installed settings and checks structural wiring, existence, regular/executable identity, protected realpath and pinned version of every bound uv/Python/node binary before executing version probes. Refuse escapes and worker/cache roots; consume verified realpaths without rediscovery.

Use the hook's trusted environment and isolated/offline uv/Python options for this mode, adding pre-cached --with pyyaml before --python; there is no network or cache-provisioning fallback. The trusted caller supplies an absolute UV_CACHE_DIR: main-checkout .agents/worklog/uv-cache for orchestrator/deploy runs, launcher-supplied <worktree>/.uv-cache for workers, temporary cache for tests. Both parent and child refuse absent or relative caches; do not derive a default in the child. Deployment procedure for the operator, not the worker: after make update and the parent's pre-exec uv identity check, perform the one-time online seed of that same private cache, `UV_CACHE_DIR=<private cache> <abs uv> run --no-config --no-project --with pyyaml python -c "import yaml"`. Record this provisioning command verbatim with expanded bound paths, its output and actual exit status in validation.md, and carry that seed evidence and status in the acceptance record. This explicit one-time online provisioning command precedes the isolated offline deployment commands; a failed seed blocks them, and none seeds or downloads implicitly. Then run the bound checker and its generator --check child using `<abs uv> run --no-config --no-project --isolated --offline --with pyyaml --python <abs python> python -I scripts/generate-agent-configs.py --check` for the generator, with the same private UV_CACHE_DIR and canonical repository cwd; every other bound Python child importing yaml uses the same pattern through the parent. Record each actual command and exit status and the real positive deployment result from the operator's host after make update; never run that host check in CI or claim a fixture substitutes for it.

Hook execution renders a fixture manifest bound to real uv: inspect shutil.which("uv"), reject a shims component before and after resolution, then search the actual mise uv install root, preferring the pinned version's directory and using the newest exact installed version only as fallback. Handle the probed platform subdirectory rather than assuming <version>/uv. Use sys.executable for real Python. Positive execution uses the canonical real installation operands rather than temporary symlink bindings, with realpath equality asserted; synthetic homes remain valid for nonexecuting renders. Use unchanged real permgate bytes and no wrapper or fake uv/Python/permgate. This positive-execution rule does not forbid the marker-writing fake uv used solely by the parent's pre-exec symlink-refusal subcase.

Execute the rendered command with a fake PermissionRequest payload under PATH=/usr/bin:/bin. Plant a .pth and usercustomize.py in the fake user site, assert neither marker executes, and assert isolated Python actually reaches permgate and returns the expected decision. Include hostile uv.toml, .venv and startup/PYTHON* inputs. Skip only this real-uv execution case, with reason real-uv-unavailable, after finding no real uv anywhere specified; a shim with a real install, invalid existing binding or execution failure is not a skip. Structural, migration and negative tests never depend on that skip.

Add regressions in the real existing classes: ClaudeManagedSettingsTest.test_trusted_entrypoint_manifest_matches_mise, test_every_bound_entrypoint_resolves_with_system_path, test_permission_request_uses_real_isolated_uv and test_trusted_entrypoint_consumers_migrate; ValidateAgentAssetsTest.test_bound_permission_request_structure and test_effective_settings_checks_binary_identity. Extend test_bound_permission_request_structure and test_trusted_entrypoint_consumers_migrate with a hostile CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR value containing spaces and a -- word, such as `project path -- injected`: only the quoted canonical raw form passes the validator and both modify-script bound classifiers; removing its quotes must fail even when shlex tokens are equal. Extend test_effective_settings_checks_binary_identity with a fixture home whose declared uv path is a symlink to a marker-writing fake uv in worker-writable storage; exercise the parent's actual pre-exec uv check, require exit 2 and trusted-launch: refused: uv-path, and assert the marker stays absent, so neither checker-side rejection nor an earlier HOME refusal counts. Keep fixture passwd-home lookup inside the test harness, without a production HOME override, discovery bypass or modification of the real installation. Also cover the generator child's exact offline --with pyyaml argv, shared private cache and successful yaml import with the real bound uv after fixture cache seeding; a base-Python-only child must not satisfy that assertion. These are subcases of the six named regressions, not skippable replacements for the structural and negative cases. Record full tests/unit/<module>.py::Class::test selectors in row-to-test evidence and run their unittest dotted equivalents red before implementation and green afterward.

Exercise the design-7 hostile PATH, hostile Git environment/configuration, worker-root path and validation-to-consumption replacement cases for the scoped mechanisms, using the common helper when available. Prove no planted payload executes; state which boundaries remain residual. No guard/delivery/state-helper descendant migration, permgate policy/source change, dependency, workflow change or worker live deployment is authorized by this task.

    50	    parser.add_argument("sweep")

    52	    parser.add_argument("--ledger")
    53	    parser.add_argument("--audit-dispositions")
    54	    args = parser.parse_args()
    55	    if not re.fullmatch(r"[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9._-]*", args.task):
    56	        parser.error(f"not a task id: {args.task!r}")
    57	    root = Path(git("-C", str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent), "rev-parse", "--show-toplevel"))
    58	    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("require_crit_review", root / "scripts/require-crit-review.py")
    59	    gate = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
    60	    spec.loader.exec_module(gate)
    61	
    62	    try:
    63	        sweep = json.loads(Path(args.sweep).read_text(), object_pairs_hook=gate.refuse_duplicate_keys)
    64	    except ValueError as error:
    65	        refuse(f"{args.sweep}: {error}")
    66	    head = git("-C", args.pr_worktree, "rev-parse", "HEAD")
    67	    if sweep.get("head_sha") != head:
    68	        refuse(f"the sweep was collected for head {sweep.get('head_sha')!r}, not {args.pr_worktree}'s HEAD {head}")
    69	
    70	    path = root / ".orchestration/acceptance" / f"{args.task}.md"
    71	    text = path.read_text() if path.is_file() else ""
    72	    record, rest = {}, text
    73	    if text.split("\n")[0] == "---":
    74	        record, error = gate.read_acceptance_record(path)
    75	        if error:
    76	            refuse(error)
    77	        lines = text.split("\n")
    78	        rest = "\n".join(lines[lines.index("---", 1) + 1 :])
    79	    replans, parts = record.get("replans", {}), ", ".join(gate.REPLAN_KEYS)
    80	    if not isinstance(replans, dict):
    81	        parser.error(f"replans must be {{<class slug>: {{{parts}}}}}; nothing written")
    82	    for cls, entry in replans.items():
